Let's design optimizer in detail.

FFT score function for unit sum:
- n = len(a)
- Choose N = next power of two >= 2n-1. For n=8192, N=16384? Actually 2n-1=16383, next power 16384. Good.
- A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
- c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:2n-1]
- Due numerical negative tiny, c=max(c,0)
- mx = c.max()
- score = 2*n*mx (since sum=1). If we don't normalize exactly, score=2*n*mx/s².
Internal FFT error small; exact slight.
Gradient for smoothed max:
- beta maybe. c values ~1/n. Scale for weights: p_k = exp(β * (c - max(c)))? Or use probabilities proportional to exp(β * c) (with max subtracted). beta in units of 1/n. As beta increases, p concentrates on max. For beta=10 n? Since c ~1e-4, β must be thousands to concentrate. Previous used beta 5 on raw c? For n=4096 c~1e-4, β=5 ineffective (weights nearly uniform); then expensive. Need set beta relative to c scale. Use c_scaled = c * n / mx? Then weights p = softmax(γ * c_scaled - max). γ 1..10. Or p ∝ exp(λ * c / c_max) with λ 1..10. Let's define lam smoothing: p = exp(lam * c / cmax - max), sum. λ starts 2, increases to 20. This concentrates moderately. For exact subgradient, λ large (100) but numerical.
- Compute grad = 2 * convolution of p with a? Need exact derivative. We can implement n-point gradient using FFT for p of length 2n-1? Easier direct for n up to 8192? Active p length 16383; direct O(n*len) too heavy. Use FFT: We want t_i =∑_{k=0}^{2n-2} p_k a_{k-i} for 0≤k-i<n. This is cross-correlation? Let's derive using convolution of p (full length) with a (length n). c_k = [a*a]_k. grad_i = ∂(∑ p_k c_k)/∂a_i = ∑ p_k (2 a_{k-i} - δ_{2i=k}a_i) with valid k. If ignore diagonal, grad_i = 2 ∑_{k} p_k a_{k-i} [0≤k-i<n]. This is 2 times convolution? Let p_full length 2n-1, a length n. conv(p, a) length 3n-2; coefficient at ? conv[p,a]_m =∑_j p_j a_{m-j}. We want for i ∑_j p_j a_{j-i}, i.e. m? Let m? If m= m0 + i? a index j-i. Set m = ? p_j a_{?}=a_{j-i}; equivalent a index = j-i => m-j=i => m=j+i. Sum over j p_j a_{m-j} with m? For fixed i, m? If m = i + j, j varies. Not constant. We want correlation of p and a with lag i: ∑_j p_j a_{j-i}. That's (a * p_reversed)? Let's index a index l = j-i => j=l+i. sum_l p_{l+i} a_l. This is cross-correlation (p with a) at lag i: (a ⋆ p)[i] =∑ l a_l p_{l+i}. This can be computed via convolution of a with reversed p_full: conv(a, rev(p)) has coeff m=∑ a_l p_{m-l}. Need p_{l+i}=p_{m-l} => m-l=l+i => m=2l+i no. Not. Actually correlation defined ∑ a_l p_{l+i}; compute using FFT: A * conj(P) with shift. Since p and a real. The FFT cross-correlation: corr_i = IFFT( A * conj(P) ) at index i? For full p length ≥n, P on N. A*conj(P) gives circular correlation ∑ a_l p_{(l+shift) mod N}. If zero padded, for shift i, gives ∑ a_l p_{l+i} (if no wrap) maybe. Let's check: DFT A_N, P_N; inverse of A * conj(P) at k = ∑ a_l p_{l-k}? Standard: IFFT(X * conj(Y))[k] = ∑ x_j y_{j-k}. We want ∑ a_l p_{l+i}; set k=-i. Since array indices modulo. So grad = 2 * corr at -i. Use phase? We can implement:
  P = np.fft.rfft(p_full, N) (zero pad full length 2n-1 to N maybe need N≥3n? For correlation no wrap, length a+p-1 =3n-2. Need N >=3n-2. But we can compute using FFT length next_fast(3n-2). For n=8192, 24574 ->32768. Still okay.
  A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
  P = np.fft.rfft(p_full, N)
  corr = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(P), N)
  This gives circular correlation with p indexed nonnegative? For valid linear, corr[i] =∑_{l=0}^{n-1} a_l p_{l+i} for i=0..2n-2. Since p length 2n-1, l+i up to 3n-3? For i up to 2n-2, l+i up to 3n-3; but p length 2n-1, many zeros. Wait we need p_{l+i}, max l n-1 + i 2n-2 =3n-3, beyond p. Algorithm:
```
def optimize_log(f0, budget, seed):
   a normalize sum1
   w=log(a) - max
   set beta/lam, lr
   best=a, best_score
   for loop until time:
       compute A,c,score
       if score < best update
       if stall: perturb/restart
       p = softmax(lam * c / cmax) # cmax maybe
       compute grad via FFT cross-correlation (Ncca next_fast(3n-1))
       grad_w = a*(grad - mean)
       Adam step w
       a = softmax(w)
       adjust lam increase slowly
```

Main optimization design in detail:
We can write a function `refine_candidate(a0, kill_time, seed, lam0, lr0)` that modifies a copy and returns best. It can take a budget seconds. It should:
```
a = normalize(a0)
w = log(a + eps) - max
m,v zeros; t=0
global_best_a,score
kill_time
stall=0
lam = lam0
lr=lr0
N = next_fast(3n-2); phase...
while time<kill_time:
   A=fft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]; clip; score=2n*max
   if score< global: update top
   # gradient
   p = softmax(lam * (c - cmax)/cmax)
   P=fft(p,N)  # p length 2n-1, zero padded N
   FR=phase*conj(A)
   conv=irfft(P*FR,N)
   grad=2*conv[n-1:2n-1]
   # Since c scaled? grad magnitude ~ sum p len? p sums1, grad ~a neighbor ~1/n, so grad_w ~1/n²? Need lr scale.
```
Scale of gradient: a_i ~1/n for base, grad_i ~O(1/n). grad - dot(a,grad) ~O(1/n), multiply a ~1/n => O(1/n²). For n=8192, ~1e-8. lr=0.1 gives dw~1e-9 too small! Previous code used grad_w = f*(grad-dot) and Adam normalizes by sqrt(v), so step size independent of gradient magnitude (Adam step ~lr). In our step, use Adam on grad_w; the division by sqrt(v) makes step O(lr). Good. If use raw gradient descent, need lr~n². Use Adam as previous. So steps about lr changes in w, affecting relative weights by lr. lr=0.05 okay.

Functions:
```
def make_mixture(n, p, H, t0, delta):
    i=np.arange(n)
    b=(i+0.5)**(-p)
    b/=b.sum()
    a=(1-H)*b
    # bump interval index range [floor(t0-delta/2), ceil(t0+delta/2)]
    l=max(0,int(floor((t0-delta/2)*n))); r=min(n-1,int(ceil((t0+delta/2)*n)))
    if r>l:
        x=np.linspace(0,1,r-l+1) # or use (i-l)/ (r-l) maybe
        shape=np.sin(np.pi*x)**2
        shape/=shape.sum()
        a[l:r+1] += H*shape
    return a
```
This mixture is discrete. We can also allow H>1? no; bump mass can exceed base if base mass negative? We restrict H∈[0,1]. To have base singular edge with H≤0.2.

Resample function:

def resample_unit(a, m):
   n=len(a)
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   f_old=a*n  # density (integrate approx1? Actually sum a=1, f_old times dx=1/n => integral≈1)
   x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old, left=f_old[0], right=f_old[-1])
   a_new=f_new / f_new.sum()? If f_new approximate density, sum f_new/m≈1; normalize at end.
   return f_new/m normalized.
```

def refine_sequence(a0, kill_time, top, best, seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    phase=...
    w=log_weights(a); m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0
    best_local=a.copy(); score=score(a)
    lam=4.0; lr=0.05; stall=0; best_seen=score
    while time<kill_time:
       batch=10+ int(lam) ?
       for step in range(batch):
          A,c,sc=score A
          if sc < best_seen: best_seen=sc; if sc<best[0] update best/a
          # if sc nonfinite reset
          cmax=c.max()
          p=softmax(lam*(c-cmax)/cmax)
          P=fft(p,N)
          FR=phase*conj(A)
          grad=2*irfft(P*FR,N)[n-1:2n-1]
          grad_w=a*(grad - np.dot(a,grad))
          a,w,m,v,t=adam(...)
          if time... break
       # stall logic: if best_seen improved since last, stall=0 else...
       # adjust lam/lr
       # every 300 steps: perturbation
    return best_local
```

In `refine_sequence`, to avoid passing huge arrays in `best` many times, `best` can be list [score, array]. It updates.
Pseudo refine:
```
def refine_sequence(a0, kill_time, top, best, seed=0, lam0=4.0, lr0=0.035):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize_unit(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<32 or n>MAX_N: ... maybe resample to 4096? But avoid.
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    phase = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*np.arange(N//2+1)*(n-1)/float(N))
    w=log_weights(a); m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lam=lam0; lr=lr0; stall=0; local_best=a.copy(); local_score=score
    # initial maybe local_score from top
    while time.monotonic() < kill_time:
       # maybe if a score not finite reset
       A,c,sc = fft_score_c(a,N)
       if not finite: reset
       if sc < local_score: local_score=sc; local_best=a.copy(); if sc<best[0]: best=[sc,a.copy()]
       # if score much worse than best and best different, maybe replace?
       cmax=c.max()
       p = softmax_lam(c,cmax,lam)
       P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
       FR = phase * np.conjugate(A)
       conv = np.fft.irfft(P*FR, N)
       grad = 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
       if not all finite: reset
       grad_w = a*(grad - np.dot(a,grad))
       a,w,m,v,t = adam_step(w, grad_w, m,v,t, lr)
       # after step, maybe no score each step? We score each loop.
       stall management update if sc < last_best etc
       if time left < 30: break
       if stall > 600: cross?
       if stall > 300: perturb
    return local_best
```
But if we score every step and do FFT for p, we compute A,c and P,FR,conv: two FFTs (a, p) + irffts. Score uses A*A irfft, gradient uses P*FR irfft. A reused. Good.
Could skip gradient step if lam too small? okay.

`softmax_lam(c,cmax,lam)`:
```
if not cmax>0: uniform
z=lam*(c-cmax)/cmax
z=np.where(finite,z,-1e300)
p=np.exp(np.minimum(z,0)) # z already ≤0
s=p.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: p[0]=1? Actually if cmax max, z[imax]=0, p=max1 so s>0.
return p/s
```
Need p length 2n-1. If c contains NaN, reset.

`fft_score_c(a,N)`:
```
n=a.size
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
if c.size and c.min()<0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
mx=c.max()
score=2*n*mx if finite else INF
return A,c,score
```

Now, cross update function:

def cross_update(a, N, phase, lam, kill_time):
   n=len(a)
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,N); FR=phase*conj(F)
   b=a.copy(); wb=log_weights(b); mb=zeros; vb=zeros; tb=0; local_best=a.copy(); sc=score_current
   steps=0
   while time<kill_time and steps<40:
       B=np.fft.rfft(b,N); h=irfft(F*B)[:2n-1]; clip
       hmax=h.max(); if hmax<=0 reset
       p=softmax_lam(h,hmax,lam*0.8)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,N); conv=irfft(P*FR,N); grad=conv[n-1:2n-1]
       grad_w=b*(grad-dot(b,grad))
       b,wb,mb,vb,tb=adam(... lr=0.05)
       steps++
   # blends
   for tt in (0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0): cand=...
```

`make_bump_add(a,n,h,t,δ)`:
```
l=max(0, int(math.floor((t-δ/2)*n)))
r=min(n-1, int(math.ceil((t+δ/2)*n)))
if r>l:
  m=r-l+1
  # use smooth sin^2
  if m>1:
     u=np.linspace(0,1,m)
     shape=np.sin(np.pi*u)**2
  else shape=np.array([1.0])
  shape/=shape.sum()
  a[l:r+1]+=h*shape
return a
```

Let's detail `refine_sequence` with state and cross. Need avoid too many large array copies? `local_best=a.copy()` only on improvement. When updating `best[1]`, copy. Avoid copying a every step.

Pseudo code refine:
```
def refine_sequence(a0, kill_time, top, best, seed, lam0=4.0, lr0=0.035):
    time_start=time.monotonic()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a0))... return
    a=normalize_unit(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<16: ... maybe expand to 2048
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    phase=...
    w=log_weights(a)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lam=lam0; lr=lr0
    # initial score
    A,c,sc=fft_score_c(a,N)
    if not finite: return
    local_score=sc; local_best=a.copy()
    if sc < best[0]: best[0]=sc; best[1]=a.copy(); top=add_top(top,a,sc)
    last_improve=sc; stall=0; cross_used=0; step=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < kill_time:
       # ensure finite
       A,c,sc=fft_score_c(a,N)
       if not finite:
           a=best[1].copy(); recompute; reset state
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if sc < local_score:
           local_score=sc; local_best=a.copy(); update best if
           stall=0; lr=min(0.12, lr*1.05)
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall>20: lr=max(0.012, lr*0.985)
       if stall > 400 and cross_used < 6 and time.monotonic() > kill_time - 300:
           new_a,new_sc = cross_update(a, N, phase, lam, 60, seed+step)
           if new_sc < local_score:
               a=new_a.copy(); local_score=new_sc; local_best=a.copy(); update best; reset state; stall=0; cross_used+=1; continue
       if stall > 250:
           # restart
           if sc > best[0] + 0.03 or (step>100 and rng.random()<0.15):
              a=best[1].copy()
           else:
              base=a
              w=log_weights(base)
              # add one or two Gaussian bumps to log weights
              nb=rng.integers(1,3)
              for _ in range(nb): amp=rng.normal(0,0.25); cn=rng.uniform(0.1,0.9); sd=rng.uniform(0.02,0.25); sign maybe
                  w+=amp*np.exp(-0.5*((idx/n-cn)/sd)**2)
              a=softmax(w)
           reset state (w,m,v,t) lam=random [2,8], lr=0.03, stall=0
       # gradient step
       p=softmax_lam(c,cmax,lam)
       P=rfft(p,N)
       FR=phase*conj(A)
       conv=irfft(P*FR,N)
       grad=2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
       if not all finite: reset; continue
       grad_w=a*(grad - np.dot(a,grad))
       a,w,m,v,t=adam_step(a,w,grad_w,m,v,t,lr)
       step+=1
       # slow lam increase if improving
       if step % 100 ==0:
          lam=min(30.0, lam*1.03)
       if time.monotonic()-last_print>60: print local
    return local_best
```

`adam_step` function:
```
def adam_step(a, w, grad_w, m, v, t, lr):
    t+=1; b1=0.9;b2=0.999
    m=b1*m+(1-b1)*grad_w
    v=b2*v+(1-b2)*grad_w*grad_w
    mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
    update=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
    update=np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(update), update,0), -5,5)
    w_new=w - lr*update
    w_new=np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new,0)
    w_new-=(np.max(w_new))
    a_new=softmax(w_new)
    return a_new,w_new,m,v,t
```
If grad_w all zero, fine. Softmax stable:
```
w -= max; e=exp(w); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; return e/s
```
Need avoid memory? okay.

Let's write `cross_update` more concretely:
```
def cross_update(a, N, phase, lam, sub_deadline, current_score):
    n=a.size
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    FR=phase*np.conjugate(F)
    b=a.copy()
    wb=log_weights(b)
    mb=np.zeros(n); vb=np.zeros(n); tb=0
    lr=0.05; lamc=max(1.0,lam*0.6)
    best_b=a.copy(); best_sc=current_score
    # We could update b and score after each step? Expensive. Do after K steps.
    for step in range(30):
       if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline: break
       B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
       h=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)[:2*n-1]
       if h.size and h.min()<0: h=np.maximum(h,0)
       hm=float(h.max())
       if not finite or hm<=0: break
       p=softmax_lam(h,hm,lamc)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(P*FR,N)
       grad=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
       if not all finite: break
       grad_w=b*(grad - np.dot(b,grad))
       b,wb,mb,vb,tb=adam_step(b,wb,grad_w,mb,vb,tb,lr)
       if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline: break
       # optional score after 10 steps
       if step % 10 == 9:
          sc=score_fft(b) # uses dynamic N? we can use Nscore dynamic. But b length same.
          if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_b=b.copy()
    # blends
    for tt in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       cand=(1-tt)*a + tt*b
       s=cand.sum(); if finite: cand/=s
       sc=score_fft(cand)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_b=cand.copy()
    return best_b, best_sc
```
This calls `score_fft` which computes Nscore; okay rare.

In refine, `cross_update` sub_deadline = kill_time - 300? To allow final. Use `cross_deadline = min(kill_time-300, time.monotonic()+60)`. If it returns improvement, adopt.

`resample_unit`:
```
def resample_unit(a,m):
   a=normalize_unit(a); n=a.size
   if n==m: return a.copy()
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   f_old=a*n
   x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old, left=f_old[0], right=f_old[-1])
   f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0)
   s=f_new.sum()
   if s<=0: f_new=np.ones(m)
   else f_new/=f_new.sum()/m? Wait f_new is density; to make probability sum1: a_new = f_new / (n? Let's derive: a_i old = f_old(i/n)*(1/n) approximately. For new length m, a_new = f_new * (1/m) if density integrates 1. Since f_old = n a, integral approx sum f_old/m_old = sum a=1. Interpolated f_new approximate same area. So a_new = f_new / m? Then sum≈(1/m)∑f_new, not exactly. We can just normalize a_new = f_new/m. Equivalently normalize f_new to sum m. Return f_new / f_new.sum() * m? Let's do:
   a_new = f_new / float(m)
   s=a_new.sum(); if s>0 a_new/=s
```
This preserves density. Good.

if not finite or s<=0: arr=np.ones(max(1,arr.size))/max(1,arr.size)
else: arr/=s
return arr
```

```
def generate_candidates(kill_time, seed, top, best):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
       for scale_n in [None, 4096, MAX_N]: add
       # perturb parent
    lengths=[]; for L in [2048,4096,MAX_N]: if L>1024 add
    for n in lengths:
       logi=np.log(np.arange(n)+0.5); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
       Nsc=next_pow2(2*n-1)
       # add parent length n? If parent size==n already added; resample to n
       # pure powers
       for p in (0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6): a=make_base; add
       # deterministic mixtures

`add_top`: Need max_items maybe 32. If score not finite return.
```
def add_top(top,a,score,max_items=32):
   if score is None or not np.isfinite(score): return top
   acopy=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(acopy)) or acopy.size==0: return top
   worst_idx=0; worst=inf
   for i,(s,_) in enumerate(top): if s>worst: worst=s; worst_idx=i
   if len(top)<max_items or score<worst:
      if len(top)<max_items: top.append((score,acopy))
      else: top[worst_idx]=(score,acopy)
   return top
```
When top empty, loop skip, append. Good.

```
def add_bump(a, h, t, delta, n):
    if h <= 0: return
    l = int(max(0.0, (t - 0.5*delta)*n))
    r = int(min(float(n-1), (t + 0.5*delta)*n))
    if r < l: r=l
    m=r-l+1
    if m <= 1:
       a[l]+=h
    else:
       u=(np.arange(m, dtype=float)+0.5)/float(m)
       shape=np.sin(np.pi*u)**2
       # ensure shape positive; if all zero? sin zero only if m=1? no
       ss=shape.sum()
       if ss <= 0 or not finite: shape=np.ones(m)/m
       else: shape/=ss
       a[l:r+1]+=h*shape
```
If interval exactly boundary, m maybe. Use float(n-1).

def finalize_sequence(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(64)
   arr=np.where(finite,arr,0); arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   n=arr.size; s=arr.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: arr=np.ones(n)
   scale=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
   arr=arr*scale/float(arr.sum())
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s2=arr.sum()
   if s2 < 0.01: arr=arr*(0.01/s2)
   return [float(x) for x in arr]

def refine_sequence(a0, kill_time, top, best, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize_unit(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n < 16:
        a=np.ones(2048) / 2048; n=2048
    if n > MAX_N:
        a=resample_unit(a, MAX_N); n=MAX_N

    w=log_weights(a); m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    A,c,sc=fft_score_c(a,N)

    local_score=sc; local_best=a.copy()

    lr=0.035; lam=4.0; stall=0; step=0; cross_used=0; last_print=time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < kill_time:

       if not finite:
           a=np.asarray(best[1],float).copy(); local_score=best[0]; w=log_weights(a); m...; A,c,sc=...; continue
       cmax=float(np.max(c))
       if sc < local_score:
           local_score=sc; local_best=a.copy(); update_best(best,sc,a); top=add_top(top,a,sc)
           stall=0
           if lr < 0.10: lr *= 1.03
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall > 50:
               lr = max(0.012, lr * 0.985)
       if step > 200 and sc > best[0] + 0.05:
           # reset to best maybe before gradient
           if rng.random() < 0.2:
              a=np.asarray(best[1],float).copy(); w=log_weights(a); m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; stall=0; lr=0.035; lam=4.0; continue
       if stall > 600 and cross_used < 8 and (kill_time - time.monotonic()) > 300:
           sub_kill=min(kill_time-300, time.monotonic()+45)
           new_a,new_sc = cross_update(a,N,phase,max(2,lam*0.7), sub_kill, sc)
           cross_used += 1
           if new_sc < local_score:
               a=new_a.copy(); local_score=new_sc; local_best=a.copy(); update_best...; w=log_weights(a); m=zeros...; stall=0; lr=0.04; lam=3.0; continue
       if stall > 350:

               a=np.asarray(best[1],float).copy()

               a=a.copy()
           w=log_weights(a)
           # apply one/two Gaussian log bumps

           for _ in range(nb):

               if amp == 0: continue

               sd=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.25))
               w += amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((idx / float(n) - center) / sd) ** 2)
           a=softmax(w)
           m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
           lam=float(rng.uniform(2.0,8.0)); lr=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.05)); stall=0; last_good? continue? We need recompute score next loop.
```

Let's write `cross_update` fully with top-level `score_fft`:
```
def cross_update(a, N, phase, lam, sub_deadline, current_score):
    n=a.size
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    FR=phase*np.conjugate(F)
    b=a.copy()
    wb=log_weights(b); mb=np.zeros(n); vb=np.zeros(n); tb=0
    lr=0.06; lamc=max(2.0, lam*0.7)
    best_b=a.copy(); best_sc=current_score
    # We need a local `last_b_checkpoint`? not.
    for st in range(36):
       if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline: break
       B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
       h=np.fft.irfft(F*B, N)[:2*n-1]
       if h.size:
          if float(np.min(h))<0: h=np.maximum(h,0)
          hm=float(np.max(h))
       else: hm=0
       if not np.isfinite(hm) or hm <=0: break
       p=softmax_lam(h,hm,lamc)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(P*FR,N)
       grad=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       gdot=float(np.dot(b,grad))
       grad_w=b*(grad-gdot)
       b,wb,mb,vb,tb=adam_step(b,wb,grad_w,mb,vb,tb,lr)
       if st % 8 == 7:
          sc=score_fft(b)
          if finite and sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_b=b.copy()
    # blends
    bl = (0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0)
    for tt in bl:
       if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline: break
       cand=(1.0-tt)*a + tt*b
       s=float(np.sum(cand))
       if not finite or s<=0: continue
       cand /= s
       sc=score_fft(cand)
       if finite and sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_b=cand.copy()
    return best_b, best_sc
```
If current_score is worse than initial a? It says current. Good.

Note: cross_update uses `score_fft` which recomputes Nscore; if best_b is b after 8 steps, maybe not exact; okay.

`softmax` stable:
```
def softmax(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,float)
    if w.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0)
    w-=float(np.max(w))
    e=np.exp(w)
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones(w.size)/w.size
    return e/s
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a, a))/(sum(a)**2)

The program builds candidates from endpoint-singular mixture families
(a base power law plus smooth interior bump(s)), then refines the best
sequences with a smoothed-L2 / Lagrange-style projected log-gradient
descent using FFT-based convolutions.  A shorter auxiliary convex
subproblem (minimize max(f*b) for fixed f) is used occasionally as a
restart.  The final sequence is scale-invariant; it is returned scaled
to sum sqrt(2*n), clipped to the evaluator's valid range.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

EPS = 1e-300
INF = float("inf")
MAX_N = 8192
MAX_EXACT_N = 8192


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def sanitize_vector(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    return arr


def normalize_unit(a):
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size if arr.size > 0 else 1, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size if arr.size > 0 else 1)
    return arr / s


def log_weights(a):
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, EPS)
    w = np.log(arr)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    return w


def softmax(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    w = w - mx
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    return e / s


def fft_score_cov(a, N):
    """Return (A, c, score) for linear convolution score (scale invariant)."""
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, None, INF
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None, None, INF
    A = np.fft.rfft(arr, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[: 2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = INF
    return A, c, score


def score_fft(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    return fft_score_cov(arr, N)[2]


def exact_score_from_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or n > MAX_EXACT_N:
        return INF
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if ev is None:
        return INF
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = float(ev(seq))
        return val if np.isfinite(val) else INF
    except Exception:
        return INF


def add_top(top, a, score, max_items=32):
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return top
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return top
    worst_idx = 0
    worst = INF
    for i in range(len(top)):
        if top[i][0] > worst:
            worst = float(top[i][0])
            worst_idx = i
    if len(top) < max_items or score < worst:
        entry = (float(score), arr.copy())
        if len(top) < max_items:
            top.append(entry)
        else:
            top[worst_idx] = entry
    return top


def score_key(item):
    return float(item[0])


def update_best(best, score, a):
    if score is not None and np.isfinite(score) and score < best[0]:
        best[0] = float(score)
        best[1] = sanitize_vector(a).copy()


def add_candidate(top, best, a):
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return INF
    h = score_fft(arr)
    if np.isfinite(h):
        add_top(top, arr, h)
        update_best(best, h, arr)
    return h


def load_parent():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 16:
        return None
    arr = sanitize_vector(arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size > MAX_N:
        arr = resample_unit(arr, MAX_N)
    elif arr.size < 1024:
        arr = resample_unit(arr, 2048)
    return arr


def resample_unit(a, m):
    a = normalize_unit(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    f_old = a * float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    f_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old, left=f_old[0], right=f_old[-1])
    f_new = np.where(np.isfinite(f_new), f_new, 0.0)
    f_new = np.maximum(f_new, 0.0)
    out = f_new / float(m)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return out / s


def make_base(logi, p):
    b = np.exp(float(p) * logi)  # logi is negative or positive? use sign below
    # Safer: logi = log(i+0.5), so exp(-p*logi)
    b = np.exp(-float(p) * logi)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(logi.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(logi.size)
    return b / s


def add_bump(a, h, t, delta, n):
    if h <= 0.0:
        return
    lo = int(max(0.0, (float(t) - 0.5 * float(delta)) * float(n)))
    hi = int(min(float(n - 1), (float(t) + 0.5 * float(delta)) * float(n)))
    if hi < lo:
        hi = lo
    m = hi - lo + 1
    if m <= 1:
        a[lo] += h
        return
    u = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    shape = np.sin(np.pi * u) ** 2
    s = float(shape.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        shape = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        shape = shape / s
    a[lo:hi + 1] += float(h) * shape


def make_mixture_one(logi, p, H, t0, delta):
    n = logi.size
    a = make_base(logi, p)
    H = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(H)))
    if H < 1.0:
        a *= (1.0 - H)
    else:
        a.fill(0.0)
    if H > 0.0:
        add_bump(a, H, t0, delta, n)
    return normalize_unit(a)


def make_mixture_multi(logi, p, H, bumps):
    n = logi.size
    a = make_base(logi, p)
    H = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(H)))
    if H < 1.0:
        a *= (1.0 - H)
    else:
        a.fill(0.0)
    for hh, tt, dd in bumps:
        if hh > 0.0:
            add_bump(a, hh, tt, dd, n)
    return normalize_unit(a)


def make_modulated(logi, p, amp, ph, off, x):
    n = logi.size
    a = np.exp(-float(p) * logi)
    mod = 1.0 + float(off) + 0.5 * float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (x + float(ph)))
    a *= mod
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return normalize_unit(a)


def generate_candidates(kill_time, seed, top, best):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(top, best, parent)
        for target in (4096, MAX_N):
            if target >= 1024 and target != parent.size:
                a = resample_unit(parent, target)
                add_candidate(top, best, a)
        pw = log_weights(parent)
        for sig in (0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.50):
            a = softmax(pw + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=parent.size))
            add_candidate(top, best, a)

    lengths = [2048, 4096, MAX_N]
    lengths = [L for L in lengths if L >= 1024]

    for n in lengths:
        logi = np.log(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5)
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

        for p in (0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.68):
            add_candidate(top, best, make_base(logi, p))

        # Promising deterministic mixture grid.
        for p in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
            for H in (0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.28, 0.45):
                for t0 in (0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80):
                    for dd in (0.04, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
                        a = make_mixture_one(logi, p, H, t0, dd)
                        add_candidate(top, best, a)

        mode_rand = 600 if n == 2048 else (1200 if n < MAX_N else 1600)
        for _ in range(mode_rand):
            if time.monotonic() > kill_time:
                break
            p = float(rng.uniform(0.32, 0.70))
            H = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.65))
            if rng.random() < 0.70:
                t0 = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
                dd = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.55))
                a = make_mixture_one(logi, p, H, t0, dd)
            else:
                t1 = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
                t2 = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
                d1 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.45))
                d2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.45))
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
                h1 = H * frac
                h2 = H - h1
                a = make_mixture_multi(logi, p, H, [(h1, t1, d1), (h2, t2, d2)])
            add_candidate(top, best, a)

        for _ in range(150):
            if time.monotonic() > kill_time:
                break
            p = float(rng.uniform(0.40, 0.62))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            off = float(rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.35))
            a = make_modulated(logi, p, amp, ph, off, x)
            add_candidate(top, best, a)

    top.sort(key=score_key)
    return top, best


def softmax_lam(c, cmax, lam):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    z = float(lam) * (c - cmax) / cmax
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -745.0)
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def adam_step(w, grad_w, m, v, t, lr):
    t += 1
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    g = np.asarray(grad_w, dtype=np.float64)
    m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
    v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
    mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
    vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
    delta = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
    delta = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, 0.0)
    delta = np.clip(delta, -6.0, 6.0)
    w_new = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64) - float(lr) * delta
    w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    w_new -= float(np.max(w_new))
    a_new = softmax(w_new)
    return a_new, w_new, m, v, t


def add_gaussian_log(w, idx, n, amp, center, sd):
    sd = max(float(sd), 1e-3)
    zz = -0.5 * ((idx / float(n) - float(center)) / sd) ** 2
    zz = np.maximum(zz, -50.0)
    w += float(amp) * np.exp(zz)


def cross_update(a, N, phase, lam, sub_deadline, current_score):
    n = len(a)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)

    b = a.copy()
    wb = log_weights(b)
    mb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tb = 0
    lr = 0.06
    lamc = max(2.0, float(lam) * 0.7)
    best_b = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    for st in range(36):
        if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline:
            break
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(F * B, N)
        h = h[: 2 * n - 1].real
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        hm = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(hm) or hm <= 0.0:
            break
        p = softmax_lam(h, hm, lamc)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * FR, N)
        grad = conv[n - 1: 2 * n - 1].real
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gdot = float(np.dot(b, grad))
        grad_w = b * (grad - gdot)
        b, wb, mb, vb, tb = adam_step(wb, grad_w, mb, vb, tb, lr)

        if st % 8 == 7:
            sc = score_fft(b)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_b = b.copy()

    blend_vals = (0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0)
    for tt in blend_vals:
        if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline:
            break
        cand = (1.0 - float(tt)) * a + float(tt) * b
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        sc = score_fft(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_b = cand.copy()
    return best_b, best_sc


def refine_sequence(a0, kill_time, top, best, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize_unit(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16:
        a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        n = 2048
    if n > MAX_N:
        a = resample_unit(a, MAX_N)
        n = a.size

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    phase = np.exp((-2j * np.pi) * (np.arange(N // 2 + 1) * (n - 1) / float(N)))
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = log_weights(a)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    A, c, sc = fft_score_cov(a, N)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return normalize_unit(best[1] if best[1] is not None else a)

    local_score = float(sc)
    local_best = a.copy()
    update_best(best, local_score, a)
    add_top(top, a, local_score)

    lr = 0.035
    lam = 4.0
    stall = 0
    step = 0
    cross_used = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < kill_time:
        A, c, sc = fft_score_cov(a, N)
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or A is None:
            fb = best[1] if best[1] is not None else local_best
            a = normalize_unit(fb)
            w = log_weights(a)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lam = 4.0
            lr = 0.035
            stall = 0
            continue

        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cmax_scaled = max(1e-300, cmax)

        if sc < local_score:
            local_score = float(sc)
            local_best = a.copy()
            update_best(best, sc, a)
            add_top(top, a, sc)
            stall = 0
            lr = min(0.10, lr * 1.03)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 50:
                lr = max(0.012, lr * 0.985)

        if step > 250 and sc > best[0] + 0.05 and rng.random() < 0.25:
            a = normalize_unit(best[1] if best[1] is not None else local_best)
            w = log_weights(a)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lam = 4.0
            lr = 0.035
            stall = 0
            continue

        if stall > 700 and cross_used < 8 and (kill_time - time.monotonic()) > 300:
            sub_deadline = min(kill_time - 300, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            new_a, new_sc = cross_update(a, N, phase, max(2.0, lam * 0.7), sub_deadline, sc)
            cross_used += 1
            if np.isfinite(new_sc) and new_sc < local_score:
                a = normalize_unit(new_a)
                local_score = float(new_sc)
                local_best = a.copy()
                update_best(best, local_score, a)
                add_top(top, a, local_score)
                w = log_weights(a)
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t = 0
                lam = 3.0
                lr = 0.04
                stall = 0
                continue

        if stall > 350:
            if sc > best[0] + 0.02 or (step > 700 and rng.random() < 0.25) or stall > 900:
                a = normalize_unit(best[1] if best[1] is not None else local_best)
            else:
                a = a.copy()
            w = log_weights(a)
            nb = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
            for _ in range(nb):
                amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
                center = float(rng.uniform(0.06, 0.94))
                sd = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.25))
                add_gaussian_log(w, idx, n, amp, center, sd)
            a = softmax(w)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            lam = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0))
            lr = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.05))
            stall = 0
            continue

        p = softmax_lam(c, cmax_scaled, lam)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        FR = phase * np.conjugate(A)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * FR, N)
        grad = 2.0 * conv[n - 1: 2 * n - 1].real
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            a = local_best.copy()
            w = log_weights(a)
            m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0
            stall = 0
            continue

        gdot = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        grad_w = a * (grad - gdot)
        a, w, m, v, t = adam_step(w, grad_w, m, v, t, lr)
        step += 1

        if step % 120 == 0:
            lam = min(35.0, lam * 1.04)

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 60.0:
            print("refine len=%d local=%.8f best=%.8f" % (n, local_score, best[0]))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return local_best


def finalize_sequence(a):
    arr = sanitize_vector(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    arr = arr * (scale / s)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
            arr = arr * (0.01 / s2)
        else:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            arr *= 0.01 / float(n)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    for _ in range(len(arr)):
        pass
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(max(0.0, budget), 2395.0)
    deadline = start + max(60.0, budget - 25.0)

    top = []
    best = [INF, None]

    gen_kill = min(start + 180.0, deadline - 320.0)
    top, best = generate_candidates(gen_kill, int(seed), top, best)

    if best[1] is None:
        fb = make_base(np.log(np.arange(4096, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5), 0.5)
        best[0] = score_fft(fb)
        best[1] = fb
        add_top(top, fb, best[0])

    top.sort(key=score_key)

    # Make candidates at full MAX_N by resampling a few short bests.
    for sc, arr in top[:8]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 200:
            break
        if arr.size < MAX_N and arr.size >= 256:
            a2 = resample_unit(arr, MAX_N)
            add_candidate(top, best, a2)
    top.sort(key=score_key)

    print("initial best score=%.8f len=%d" % (best[0], len(best[1])))

    # Main optimization stages.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220:
        killA = min(deadline - 220, start + 720.0)
        best_a = refine_sequence(best[1], killA, top, best, int(seed))
        top.sort(key=score_key)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220 and len(top) > 1:
        a2 = top[1][1]
        killB = min(deadline - 220, time.monotonic() + 420.0)
        best_b = refine_sequence(a2, killB, top, best, int(seed) + 101)
        top.sort(key=score_key)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        refine_sequence(best[1] if best[1] is not None else top[0][1], deadline - 120, top, best, int(seed) + 202)
    top.sort(key=score_key)

    print("search best score=%.8f len=%d" % (best[0], len(best[1])))

    # Exact validation for a few top candidates, throttled.
    exact_best_score = INF
    exact_best_a = None
    exact_order = []
    if best[1] is not None:
        exact_order.append(best[1])
    for sc, arr in top[:6]:
        exact_order.append(arr)

    exact_done = 0
    for arr in exact_order:
        exact_done += 1
        if exact_done > 4:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35.0:
            break
        if arr is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > MAX_EXACT_N:
            continue
        sc_ex = exact_score_from_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc_ex):
            if sc_ex < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = float(sc_ex)
                exact_best_a = sanitize_vector(arr).copy()
            print("exact candidate score=%.8f len=%d" % (sc_ex, arr.size))
            if exact_best_score < best[0] - 5e-4:
                break

    if exact_best_a is not None and np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        final = exact_best_a
        final_score = exact_best_score
    else:
        final = best[1] if best[1] is not None else top[0][1]
        final_score = best[1] is not None and best[0] is not None and np.isfinite(best[0]) and best[0] or INF

    print("selected final \"score\"=%.8f" % final_score)

    try:
        out = finalize_sequence(final)
    except Exception:
        out = finalize_sequence(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))

    # Very last sanity: if evaluator is available and time permits, evaluate returned object.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
            if ev is not None:
                val = float(ev(out))
                print("returned evaluate_sequence=%.8f" % val)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
